##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_avfi_variable_map_v0.md (21651 bytes)
# SMH-AVFI Causal and Variable Map v0.1

Status: CONSOLIDATION + SPECIFICATION DOCUMENT (12 Nov 2026, main conversation, session 59, PROGRESS-block-adjacent; see the KNOWLEDGE.md session-59 entry). Built answering the SMH-x-AVFI master research prompt (the deep-research brief canvas of the same conversation is the user-facing synthesis; this file is the durable programme artifact). No new computation, no new number beyond those frozen elsewhere; every measured value cited is a frozen, selftest-asserted design constant from the pre-2021 UNSEALED sample; the NQ 2021+ holdout is SPENT and never touched; the forward log stays the only proof object. The AVFI v3.4 source (Pine v5, measurement edition, uploaded Nov 2026) was studied line by line; the four uploaded screenshots (Nikkei 225 weekly and monthly, DAX weekly, NQ weekly with wave annotations) were analyzed by vision model in the companion session and carry no evidential weight here beyond hypothesis generation.

## 1. Scope and governing principles

SMH comes first. The strategy layer-cake is: state, then event probability, then precursors, then direction, then microstructure, then execution. AVFI enters at layer 3 as a CANDIDATE measurement system and must demonstrate incremental information beyond the SMH state and baseline models before receiving any meaningful role; the record of that demonstration attempt is closed and mostly null (section 6). Inevitability is conditional hazard, never dated certainty: the formal object is P(significant event within h | condition) and its Kaplan-Meier clock, against a matched do-nothing climatology, with pre-declared kill lines. The six foundational properties and their operational uses are frozen in the strategy document (laws 1-6: the clock, sigma-unit thresholds, the trend licence, volume confirmation, state-dependent hazard, decay discipline) and are publicly anchored in the companion brief (Cont 2001 stylized facts; Moskowitz-Ooi-Pedersen 2012; Clark 1973 and the transaction-clock line; Filimonov-Sornette 2012; McLean-Pontiff 2016; the square-root impact literature).

## 2. The AVFI v3.4 dependency map (from source, verbatim semantics)

```
r  = log(hlc3_t / hlc3_{t-1})
sigmaR  = sqrt(pi/2) * EWM(|r|, hl=30)                        volatility floor A
sigmaPk = sqrt(EWM(log(H/L)^2, hl=30) / (4 ln 2))              volatility floor B (Parkinson)
sigma3  = sqrt(max(sigmaR^2, sigmaPk^2))                       combined floor
z   = r / (sigma3 + eps)                                      vol-scaled return
w   = tanh(k * max(0, |z| - deadzone)) * sign(z)              directional weight (k=1, dz=0.2)
va  = exp(EWM(log(vol), hl=65))[1]                             volume baseline (prior bar)
rho = vol / (va + eps)                                         volume surprise
m3  = rho / (1 + rho/gamma)                                    soft-capped participation (gamma=2.5)
dv3   = w * m3 * va                                            flow innovation
flow3 = EWM(dv3, hl=65); avfi3 = EMA(flow3/va, 3)             AVFI v3
--- v2 chain: sigmaR only; vave2 = EWM(vol, hl=65)[1]; rho2, m2 analogous
flow2 = EWM(w2*m2*vave2, hl=65); avfi2 = EMA(flow2/vave2, 3)
emaV2 = EMA(avfi2, 105)                                        slow baseline
spread = avfi3 - emaV2                                         MODEL DISAGREEMENT (not a price spread)
sp_z   = (spread - SMA(spread,500)) / stdev(spread,500,ddof=1) rolling z (current bar included)
sp_rz  = (spread - med[1]) / ((q75[1]-q25[1])/1.349)           robust z, PRIOR-bar window (no self-inclusion)
newHigh/newLow = fresh 20-week closing extremes
divTop/divBot  = newHigh/low with sp_rz <= 0 / >= 0            H8 flags (tested null)
climaxTop/Bot  = rho >= 1.5 with |z| <= deadzone at fresh extreme  H9 flags (tested null)
CUSUM   = one-sided on spread vs mu_s[1], k=0.5, h=4 sd; 13-week flag hold   regime-break guard
--- v3.4 layers (feed nothing back):
varGk = max(0.5*ln(H/L)^2 - (2ln2-1)*ln(C/O)^2, 0); sigmaGK = sqrt(EWM(varGk, hl=30))
clv   = (2C - H - L)/(H-L) in [-1,1]
dv3Clv = w3 * m3 * va * (1 + 0.5*clv)   -> parallel chain avfi3_clv -> spread_clv -> sp_z_clv
asym  = sigma_dn / sigma_up  (separate EWMs of |r| over down/up weeks)         descriptive only
--- execution block: mom12 = log(C/C[-12]); vol8 = 8w sample stdev * sqrt(52);
U3 size = min(1, 0.20/vol8) * (0.5 if reversal_down else 1) when tLong
```

Key structural facts: every series is a backward-looking EWM (halflives 30/65, EMA 3 and 105, normalization 500); the spread is near-integrated (lag-1 ~0.92-0.99); flags are contemporaneous bar-close objects; the v3.4 layers are deliberately non-feedback (observation only).

## 3. Variable-by-variable: the six questions

Family answers shared by the whole flow chain. What it contains: a vol-gated, volume-weighted, slowly normalized measure of directional participation intensity. What it does not contain: aggressor order flow, order-book imbalance, institutional positioning, or anything faster than the EWM halflives allow. Contemporaneous vs anticipatory: contemporaneous by construction; any anticipatory content could only be CONDITIONAL (the variable's value changing the probability of a future event), never mechanical, and must be demonstrated by a pre-registered event test against persistence-preserving nulls.

| Variable | Measures | Timing | Research role | Record status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| sigma3, sigmaGK, sigma3GK | Conditional weekly vol scale (range and OHLC floors) | Contemporaneous | State / risk context | In production via vol8 targeting; GK layer descriptive |
| z, w | Vol-scaled return and its gated direction | Contemporaneous | Descriptive component of flow | Tested only inside failed timing variants |
| rho, m3 | Volume surprise vs own baseline | Contemporaneous | Participation proxy (law 4) | H9 climax flags tested null (n=3); event anatomy 1.138x is the positive law-4 fact |
| dv3, flow3, avfi3 | Flow innovation, its slow level, normalized | Lagging | Descriptive; throttle input | U3 throttle: drawdown control partially replicated OOS, Sharpe edge did not |
| vave2, flow2, avfi2, emaV2 | Arithmetic-baseline variant and slow trend | Lagging | Regime classification (4-state) | State system scored 0.42 vs random-entry 0.465 |
| spread | Model disagreement (two vol/volume constructions) | Lagging (near-integrated) | Regime descriptor | THE measured job: risk throttle, regime descriptor |
| sp_z | Spread position in 500w rolling units | Lagging | Descriptive; covariate candidate | sp_z covariate closed null (adds nothing); entry filter zero-trade vacuous |
| sp_rz | Robust (IQR, prior-bar) spread position | Contemporaneous | H8 input | H8 tested null: divTop n=32 wrong-signed p 0.79; divBot 0 events |
| newHigh/newLow | Fresh 20-week closing extremes | Contemporaneous | Conditioning context | Occupancy facts only; never a signal |
| divTop/divBot, climaxTop/Bot, tbFlagged | Fade and effort-vs-result flags | Contemporaneous flags | Descriptive only | Amendment 3 signed, tested once, NULL, frozen forever |
| CUSUM guard | Spread distribution break | Lagging | Observability | No hypothesis reads it |
| clv, dv3Clv, spread_clv, sp_z_clv | Result-quality-weighted flow variant | Lagging | Descriptive | H10 withdrawn unspent; never evaluated |
| asym | Down/up vol asymmetry (leverage-like) | Lagging | Descriptive only | No claim exists |
| roll/expiry windows | Contract-splice artifact risk | Calendar | Measurement hygiene | Stage 0d: 7 of 11 divergences explained |
| proxy-volume fallback | TR-based stand-in when no volume | Contemporaneous | Hygiene warning | Panel row only; semantics change flagged |
| mom12 (T) | 12-week trend state | Lagging | Directional evidence (law 3) | The one replicated effect; holdout p 0.069 at 43% power |
| vol8, volFactor, throttle, U3 size | Realized vol and sizing | Contemporaneous | Execution and risk | U3 measured job: sizing throttle |

The hypothesis each candidate could test, and the evidence bar that would establish predictive value (the six-question fields 5 and 6, stated once per candidate):

- rho / climax: hypothesis, effort without result at a fresh extreme precedes reversal (H9). Evidence bar, temporal availability at bar close plus a two-sample event test on forward returns beating the same-direction fresh-extreme control at pre-declared alpha, with family max-t across the H8/H9 family. RESULT: executed 11 Nov 2026, null (n=3 climax-at-extreme in 26 years; family max-t 0.089).
- sp_rz / divTop: hypothesis, flow fade at a fresh extreme precedes reversal (H8). Same bar. RESULT: executed, null (n=32, wrong-signed, p 0.79; bottom arm 0 events, vacuous).
- spread / sp_z: hypothesis, the model-disagreement descriptor carries state or timing information beyond vol, momentum, duration. Evidence bar, the covariate decision test (duration-only vs duration+covariate cloglog LOYO Brier against the pre-declared 2.0 percent bar, coverage declared first). RESULT: executed, adds nothing (coverage 10/41; 1.80 percent below the bar).
- sp_z_clv: hypothesis, close-location weighting improves regime quality (H10). Evidence bar as drafted in Amendment 4 (Fisher-z Spearman difference on forward 8-week realized vol, persistence-preserving shift null). RESULT: withdrawn unspent because the as-drafted within-sample bootstrap was invalid and the honest revision only reached 21-29 percent power. Never evaluated; the layer stays descriptive.
- asym: hypothesis, down/up vol asymmetry conditions event hazard or direction. Evidence bar, same covariate machinery with coverage-first declaration. RESULT: never tested; no claim exists; would require a new dated amendment (the covariate program is closed, so this is a refused cell until Andrea reopens it).
- emaV2 / 4-state regime: hypothesis, the state carries direction or timing. RESULT: measured and closed (0.42 vs random 0.465; the state machine's transition structure is measured and is the honest content, "AVFI cycles while the clock ticks; neither explains the other").

## 4. AVFI mapped to the six SMH properties

Law 1 (vol persistence and mean reversion): sigmaR/sigmaPk/GK are direct measurements; the operational object is the external sig_w clock (8-week rolling std), which shares the family; vol8 turns law 1 into sizing. Law 2 (heavy tails): the dead zone, the tanh saturation, the soft volume cap, and the IQR robust z are all tail-defensive constructions; thresholds are sigma-relative everywhere. Law 3 (horizon-dependent autocorrelation): emaV2 is a slow persistence measure; the licensed directional object is mom12 (rule set T), external to AVFI. Law 4 (volume-dependent impact): rho and the climax flags are the AVFI-side proxies; the measured weekly fact is the event anatomy's volume signature (median 1.138x, zero thin events), and the AVFI flag built on the same intuition returned null. Law 5 (self-excitation): the CUSUM guard is a crude break detector on the spread; genuine Hawkes measurement is data-blocked (Amendment 10). Law 6 (decay): every frozen null, the kill lines, and the never-re-tune rule are the programme's answer; AVFI's own history (27 dead variants) is the local case file.

## 5. Role boundaries (state vs event vs direction vs execution)

State: the sig_w clock (external), the AVFI 4-state regime, sigma3/vol8. Event: the KM clocks and the ECS trigger-event-clock triple; NO AVFI variable is an event object. Direction: mom12 and the two forward peeks; AVFI direction cells are flagged seductive and untrusted. Execution: vol8 targeting, the U3 throttle, the roll/expiry hygiene windows, cost conventions. Descriptive-only: all tops/bottoms flags, the v3.4 measurement layers, the PGT geometry layer (Amendment 5 withdrawn), the CUSUM guard. A descriptive flag is not a trading signal; this table is the standing proof that the distinction is enforced.

## 6. Leading-information analysis

The formal argument: an EWM chain cannot be mechanically anticipatory; anticipatory content can only exist as conditional structure, P(event within h | variable value) differing from P(event within h | state, vol, momentum, duration). Establishing it requires, in order: (a) temporal availability (the value exists at decision close; no repainting); (b) a pre-registered dated amendment with frozen defaults; (c) a persistence-preserving null or validated two-sample event machinery (near-integration forbids within-sample resampling, lessons 11/13); (d) family multiplicity control; (e) out-of-sample or forward confirmation. The record: every weekly-grain candidate that reached stage (b) was tested and returned null or was withdrawn unspent for power/invalidity; layer 3 is fully answered; no AVFI variable has demonstrated leading information at the weekly grain. What remains formally untested: other grains (4h and intraday: a different hypothesis on different data, refused until a data decision and a new amendment, with the honest prior from the order-flow literature that flow predictability is minutes-to-hours); other markets (the cross-market amendment transfers the CLOCK, not AVFI; an AVFI transfer would be its own amendment); and the forward-scored seductive cells (three, registered, closed register). The distinction demanded by the master prompt is settled by test: a fresh extreme, a divergence-like configuration, and a volume climax are contemporaneous descriptive flags (H8/H9 nulls); a genuine early warning would be a variable passing ladder (a)-(e); none exists today.

## 7. Risk register: look-ahead, repainting, measurement, statistical

1. Self-inclusion: plain sp_z includes the current bar in its own 500-week window (an extreme inflates its own reference); the robust variant fixed this with the prior-bar window; the registered robust z is the only flag-legal z.
2. Repainting: Pine alertconditions and plotshape markers fire intrabar unless consumed at bar close; panel rows are last-bar objects that change while the week forms; the booking tools enforce completed-bar and fetch-staleness guards; the research convention is last-completed-Friday decisions only, never the forming bar.
3. Warmup and coverage: first valid spread/z near bar 759-760 (warmup 260 + normalization 500); first valid sp_rz 2015-04-17; coverage killed the sp_z covariate (10 of 41 entries); every future covariate declares coverage FIRST (T0 gates), occupancy only, before any outcome.
4. Measurement semantics: proxy volume changes the indicator's meaning on no-volume symbols (panel row exists; exports must carry the flag); hlc3 vs close conventions differ from the clock's close-to-close returns; roll/expiry splices distort weekly bars (7 of 11 divergence weeks were roll/expiry-adjacent; the marker is observation-only, never a filter); single-vendor cross-checks (stage 0d caveat); TradingView vs pandas parity requires the ddof=1 stdev fix (v3.3.1) on every chart edition.
5. Statistical: near-integrated series invalidate within-sample resampling (lessons 11/13); the overlapping-episode climatology band is too narrow in truth (flagged; the cut favours the salience claim); episode dependence breaks the binomial kill-line approximation (pre-declared, approximate, flagged); the 2.5-sigma bar is endogenous (the event raises its own yardstick; the lone-print audit bounded the artifact and the doctrine sentence carries the definition); multiplicity is controlled by family max-t, the SPA three-bound convention, BH+BY for independent shortlists, and the closed three-cell register; small n (41/33/26 design episodes; 17/9 direction splits); one instrument, one era (trigger rate swings 4.5x across 5-year buckets; rows stable, years not); the NQ 2021+ holdout is spent and never evaluated again; visual chart configurations are hindsight unless pre-registered, and the ones visible on the uploaded screenshots are exactly the flag classes already tested to null.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_cell_promotion_critique_v0.md (10759 bytes)
# SMH seductive-cell promotion rule, critique v0

Status: THEORY-CLASS CRITIQUE under autonomy charter v1, class T, item
T-2. Written 19 Nov 2026, session 116, block 119. A documentation-level
critique of a FROZEN pre-registered rule: no market data read, no
evaluation, nothing registered, nothing booked, no tool edited. The
arithmetic below is pure binomial algebra on the rule's own constants
and on design-bookkeeping counts already in the record; the forward log
is empty and will stay empty this session, so no rule application
occurs. Any proposed change to the rule carries zero status and is
listed only in the queue's ratification section; a frozen proof rule
changes only by Andrea's dated word.

## 1. The rule as frozen

Verbatim from docs/smh_proof_criteria_v0.md (section 4, sessions
44/46; the register CLOSED at three cells):

    "Promotion rule, identical for all three: a cell is examined only
    when it holds n >= 10 forward rows; it is promoted only if its
    forward event-share has a CP95 lower bound exceeding the
    UNCONDITIONAL forward event share over the same rows. Below that,
    cells are counted silently."

Implementation facts (read from the frozen tooling, unaudited here
beyond reading): smh_gates.py CELL_MIN_N = 10; promotion is a doc
action, never automated; the CP95 band is smh_theorem.py's cp_band,
the two-sided exact Clopper-Pearson interval via Beta quantiles, so
the "lower bound" is the 2.5 percent Beta quantile. The register: 1.
mom12 <= 0 at entry (design 6/7); 2. reversal_down at entry (design
9/12); 3. priced-calm residual-fear half, VXN > 16.47 at entry (design
16/20; PC_LOW the deep-calm complement: 21 entries, 10 events, share
0.476 band [0.263, 0.690]).

## 2. Method and cross-validation of this critique's arithmetic

CP95 lower bound computed by exact binomial root-finding: the p where
P(Bin(n,p) >= k) = 0.025, stdlib Python, no data. Cross-validated
against the framework's own frozen numbers: LB(27/40) = 0.5087 > 0.5
and LB(26/40) = 0.4832 < 0.5, which reproduces the direction peek's
frozen 27/40 promotion threshold exactly; LB(6/7) = 0.4213 against
the frozen CP_MOM12NEG band (0.421, 0.857, 0.996); LB(9/12) = 0.4281
against CP_REV_DOWN (0.428, 0.750, 0.945); LB(10/10) = 0.6915 =
0.025^(1/10). First run of the script had an inverted bisection and
returned garbage; the failure was caught by the sanity anchors,
diagnosed, fixed and rerun. All numbers below are from the fixed,
anchor-validated run.

## 3. First finding: the denominator is ambiguous, and the choice
changes the rule's whole character

"THE UNCONDITIONAL FORWARD EVENT SHARE OVER THE SAME ROWS" has three
candidate readings, and the record contains all three numbers:

  R1: the pooled forward event share over all forward rows in the
      same period. Under the design expectations this is the
      compression cum12, 0.634 (sanity: the design halves pool to
      exactly (10+16)/(21+20) = 26/41 = 0.634). This is the reading
      most faithful to the words.
  R2: the matched climatology, 0.441 (frozen CLIM_CUM_12W).
  R3: the other half's share, 0.476 (frozen PC_LOW), the number the
      proof-criteria doc itself uses when motivating the rule.

The rule's rhetoric and its mechanics disagree: the doc motivates the
rule with the half-vs-half comparison "0.476 vs 0.800 at design, bands
overlapping, era-confounded", but the rule as written compares a cell
against the pooled share, not against 0.476. Under R1 the demanded
effect over the pool is 0.800 vs 0.634; under R3 it is 0.800 vs 0.476.
These are different tests with different floors and different years-
to-promotion. A pre-registered rule whose motivating example is not
the test it performs is a documentation incoherence, even before any
row exists.

## 4. The operating characteristics the rule never declared

Minimum events k needed for promotion at the n >= 10 floor, per-look
false-promotion probability when the cell is truly at the base, and
power when the cell's true share is the design 0.80 (pure binomial
arithmetic):

  n   base   kmin   fp(null)  power at 0.80
  10  0.441    8/10   0.0239   0.6778
  10  0.476    9/10   0.0072   0.3758
  10  0.634   10/10   0.0105   0.1074
  20  0.441   14/20   0.0175   0.9133
  20  0.476   15/20   0.0121   0.8042
  20  0.634   18/20   0.0084   0.2061
  40  0.441   25/40   0.0147   0.9971
  40  0.634   32/40   0.0187   0.5931
  60  0.441   35/60   0.0186   1.0000
  60  0.634   46/60   0.0203   0.7935

Readings:

F1 (per-look error is controlled). At the null the per-look false-
promotion probability is 0.7 to 2.4 percent, at or below the nominal
2.5 percent, because Clopper-Pearson is conservative. As a single
look, the rule is coherent.

F2 (the R1 floor makes design-scale cells unpromotable at small n).
Under R1 (base 0.634), a cell replicating its design share exactly
(0.80) promotes with probability 0.11 at n = 10 (needs 10/10), 0.21 at
n = 20, and only 0.59 at n = 40. The "n >= 10" floor is decorative
under R1: the real floor is roughly 30 to 40 cell rows, which at the
design trigger rate is decades. Under R3 the same cell promotes with
probability 0.38 at n = 10. The rule's severity differs by a factor
of 3 to 4 in required rows depending on the reading, which the frozen
text does not resolve.

F3 (no declared operating characteristics). Every other gate in the
proof machinery declares its error rates and power at design
parameters: the kill lines carry false-kill 0.0335/0.0282/0.0290/
0.0415 and power 0.2855/0.4465/0.7243/0.9046; the two direction peeks
carry power 0.971 and false-pass 0.018. The cell rule carries none.
This is the framework's own standard applied everywhere except here.

F4 (repeated looks, uncorrected). The two-peek direction rule allows
exactly two examinations; the cell rule re-opens at every resolved
forward row past n = 10, for three cells, with no correction across
looks or cells. Even at per-look false-promotion around one to two
percent, the cumulative family probability over a lifetime of looks
is materially above the nominal level. The mitigation is real and
stated: promotion is a doc action, never automated, and a promoted
cell means only "the doctrine's sentence may be re-armed as a
hypothesis with a measured band", never a trade change, never a
claim on the positive proof. The main proof (57 of 104) is untouched
by any cell verdict by construction. So the flaw pollutes doctrine
hygiene, not the proof. That is the honest size of it.

F5 (a design-count illustration, flagged as such). At their design
counts no cell would promote under R1: LB 0.421 (6/7), 0.428 (9/12),
0.563 (16/20), all below 0.634; under R2 only the priced-calm half
clears (0.563 > 0.441). The mom12 cell's design n = 7 sits below the
10-row examination floor entirely. Design bookkeeping only, quoted
to show the scale the rule actually demands.

## 5. Verdict

Does the pre-declared rule cohere at the cells' small n? As an
anti-seduction FILTER, yes: it is conservative per look, its error
rate at the null is controlled, and its effect (only overwhelming
cell evidence can re-open a doctrine sentence) matches the framework's
doctrine. AS A PRE-REGISTERED TEST, no, on four specific counts: the
denominator is ambiguous between numbers a factor apart in required
rows (F2/F3 in section 4); the motivating example in the doc is not
the comparison the rule performs (section 3); no operating
characteristics were declared for the only gate family that lacks
them (F3); and its re-examination structure is uncorrected repeated
looking (F4), softened only by the doc-action status.

## 6. Proposal to the ratification queue (zero status until his dated
word)

P1. Pin the denominator to R1, the pooled forward event share over
all forward rows in the same period, the reading most faithful to
the rule's own words. Kill criterion: if his word pins a different
reading, the operating-characteristics table of this doc is void and
must be recomputed under the chosen reading before any cell is first
examined.
P2. Add this doc's table (section 4) to the proof-criteria doc as
the cell rule's declared operating characteristics, so the cell family
meets the framework's own standard. Kill criterion: the declaration
dies with P1's denominator choice it was computed under.
P3 (optional, included for completeness): declare cell examinations
at pre-declared milestones only (mirroring the two-peek structure),
for example first examination at n = 20 cell rows then n = 40, to
bind the repeated-looking leak. Kill criterion: Andrea may refuse
this as over-engineering; refusing it leaves F4 standing as an
accepted, documented property.
All three die together with his single word of refusal. No urgency:
the log is empty; no cell can legally be examined for years; the
amendment window is open by design precisely because no row exists.

## 7. Honest computation log

Pure binomial arithmetic on the rule's own constants and design-
bookkeeping counts already in the record; the implementation cross-
validated against the frozen 27/40 peek threshold and the frozen cell
bands (0.421/0.428) before use; one implementation failure occurred
(inverted bisection), was caught by the sanity anchors, diagnosed,
fixed and rerun, and is recorded here rather than hidden. No market
data read, no forward row exists or was touched, nothing evaluated,
nothing registered, nothing booked, no tool edited, no frozen
constant or rule changed. The proposals in section 6 hold zero status.
Every quoted constant re-verified against the frozen record this
session apart from the arithmetic.

## 8. Citation audit

- Rule verbatim, register, PC_LOW 0.476, cells: smh_proof_criteria_v0
  lines 48-65 (sessions 44/46).
- CELL_MIN_N = 10: smh_gates.py line 117 and smh_theorem.py line 242;
  the "examined only at 10+ rows, promotion a doc action, never
  automated" framing: smh_gates_v0.md sections 1-2.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_cox_v01.md (7630 bytes)
# SMH Cox Layer v0.1 (covariates enter the hazard model)

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Same discipline as the hazard layer v0: no inferential claims, no forward statistics, no trades; the forward protocol (smh_hazard_v0.md section 5) remains the only path to trust. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 32. CORRECTED 12 Nov 2026, session 54 (PROGRESS block 62): the section-4 LOYO Brier pair (0.1574 full, 0.1596 duration-only) was found defective during the sp_z covariate study's pre-registered anchor validation - unreproducible under any construction consistent with the frozen coefficients, frozen from scratch prose with no instrument - and is superseded by the corrected pooled per-row LOYO values below; the verdict survives and strengthens. The corrected values are recomputed and asserted by the smh_spz.py selftest (verify_all's 23rd check).

## 1. What this layer asks

The hazard layer v0 measured the clock: the hazard of the first significant week rises with weeks since a compression entry. This layer asks whether entry conditions add anything ON TOP of duration: does knowing the momentum sign, the AVFI state, or the depth of compression at entry change the first-event hazard? The instrument is a discrete-time survival model (person-week cloglog hazard, the grouped-data form of Cox regression): the same mathematics used for clinical time-to-event covariates. This is the medicine bridge made operational.

## 2. The dataset (corrected)

Person-week expansion of the 41 compression episodes: each episode contributes one row per week at risk, from entry until its first significant week, right-censored at 12 weeks, at NaN flags, or at sample end. Total: 327 person-weeks, 41 episodes, 26 first events. Standing consistency check (adopted this session): sum of KM at-risk counts across horizons equals the person-week count (327 = 327, passes; the pre-correction frozen table failed this check, 343 vs 327, which is how its off-by-one was caught; correction logged in smh_hazard_v0.md).

Covariates, all frozen at entry:

- h: weeks since entry (duration, the clock itself).
- mom_neg: 1 if 12-week momentum <= 0 at entry.
- rev_dn: 1 if AVFI state at entry is reversal_down.
- depth: log(sig_w(entry)/LOW) (<= 0; more negative = deeper compression at entry).

Episode-level correlation between mom_neg and rev_dn: 0.24 (low enough to enter both).

## 3. The model (frozen numbers)

Discrete-time hazard via cloglog link on the person-week data, binomial family, cluster-robust standard errors by episode. exp(coef) is the conditional hazard ratio.

| covariate | coef | cluster SE | HR |
|---|---|---|---|
| const | -3.081 | 0.400 | - |
| h (duration) | +0.017 | 0.050 | 1.02 |
| mom_neg | +0.225 | 0.483 | 1.25 |
| rev_dn | +0.502 | 0.416 | 1.65 |
| depth | -1.596 | 1.464 | 0.20 |

Readings, honestly:

1. All three entry-condition coefficients point in the direction the framework predicts (negative prior momentum and reversal_down state raise the first-event hazard; deeper compression at entry raises it, since depth <= 0 and the coefficient is negative).
2. Every cluster-robust SE is 2-7x its coefficient. At n = 26 events, none of the entry conditions is distinguishable from noise. This is the honest size of what we know.
3. depth has the largest magnitude and the largest SE: the deepest-compression entries carry the highest measured hazard, and the least certainty about it.

## 4. The decisive test: does anything beat the clock alone?

CORRECTION (session 54; full investigation in docs/smh_spz_covariate_v0.md section 3.0): the numbers originally frozen here - leave-one-episode-out CV Brier 0.1574 full versus 0.1596 duration-only, a 1.4 percent out-of-fold gain - were frozen from session-32 scratch prose with no instrument and do NOT reproduce. With the frozen duration slope +0.0129 the out-of-fold predictions are near-constant at the pooled rate 26/327 = 0.0795, which forces a pooled per-row LOYO Brier near 0.074 (0.1596 would require near-constant predictions near 0.37), and an episode-level Brier at base 26/41 cannot mathematically fall below 0.232. The session-34 TypeScript replication independently contains no Brier and its LR columns (duration vs constant LR 0.05) already contradicted the pair.

The corrected values (pooled per-row LOYO Brier, 41 folds, all other constructions frozen as in section 3; recomputed and asserted by the smh_spz.py selftest):

- Full model (duration + all three covariates): 0.07607
- Duration-only model: 0.07406
- Intercept-only model (constant weekly hazard): 0.07364

Improvement of the full model over duration-only: MINUS 2.71 percent, out of fold - the covariates make prediction WORSE. Duration-only versus intercept-only: +0.6 percent worse as well; the weekly hazard is indistinguishable from constant at n = 41, exactly the session-34 LR reading. VERDICT, corrected and STRENGTHENED: at this sample size the hazard content of the SMH compression signature is DURATION - honestly, the clock's pooled hazard level, not even its slope - not the entry conditions. The clock is real in the design data; the covariates are, for now, decoration, and out of fold they are worse than decoration. Any claim that momentum sign, AVFI state, or compression depth improves event TIMING is speculation until the forward protocol accumulates episodes that say otherwise.

This verdict is the framework working as designed: the covariates that looked seductive in the direction table (the 6/7 cell, the reversal_down shape) do not transfer into timing power. Two different questions: direction of the first event (a 26-event conditional table, unmodeled) and timing of the first event (this layer). The forward protocol scores both, separately.

## 5. What is and is not inevitable (the inevitability ladder, theory section)

Given a compression entry, the measured pre-2021 design says:

1. INEVITABLE in the distribution sense: an event distribution with rising hazard, cumulative first-event probability 0.63 by 12 weeks, median lag 5. Roughly one entry in three sees nothing significant within a quarter. Vol expansion after compression is a law; a specific expansion within a specific quarter is a probability.
2. CONDITIONAL but UNPROVEN: the direction splits (mom12- entries resolving up 6/7; reversal_down 9/3). Small-n, selected, and explicitly flagged seductive.
3. NOT INEVITABLE, and not claimed: the direction at a chosen time (central theorem), the specific date (hazard wobbles, no peaks promised), and covariate-improved timing (this layer's null verdict).

The inevitable future is visible to no one in public form. The private observer's honest advantages are exactly three: information others do not have (informed flow), speed others do not have (infrastructure), and a calibration record others have not measured (the forward-scored log). SMH builds the third. That is the entire content of "the trade no one sees" as this programme can honestly hold it.

## 6. Next steps, in order of honesty

1. Forward protocol accumulation (smh_hazard_v0.md section 5): every new compression episode is a fresh, out-of-sample person-week set. No covariate review before 20 forward episodes exist; no inferential language before the protocol's 104 windows.
2. Direction model deferred: modeling the SIGN of the first event needs more events than 26; the seductive cells stay flagged and unmodeled.
3. Duration shape: the linear-in-h term can be tested against the KM table's own shape (rising then wobbling) on unsealed data only; low priority, the KM table already carries the shape honestly.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_cox_v01.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0.md (8572 bytes)
# SMH Cross-Market Stage A v0 (Amendment 9, SIGNED): Gold / Nikkei / DAX design bookkeeping

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on each market's PRE-2021 UNSEALED window only. Executed 2026-11-14, the same session as Andrea's recorded signature in docs/amendment9_crossmarket_smh.md, per amendment Section 5 Stage A and Section 7 (Stage A machinery authored only after sign-off: research/stage0x_stageA.py). Each market's 2021+ window is a FRESH SEALED HOLDOUT: this document computes nothing on it beyond the availability counts already recorded by the stage0x bookkeeping (307 weeks available per market). Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; every cross-market forward claim is scored only by that market's own forward rows. The NQ forward log, kill lines, calibration expectations, readouts and the 57-of-104 positive proof are untouched and never pooled with anything below.

## 1. Machinery validation (before any market was computed)

The Stage A implementation is an INDEPENDENT reimplementation of the frozen NQ conventions (entries by cross semantics, person-week construction, KM, Greenwood bands, matched climatology, kill-line rule). Hard gate: it must first reproduce the frozen NQ constants from the canonical NQ weekly file with the frozen NQ cuts. RESULT: PASS first run, all 18 checks: 41 entries, 327 person-weeks, 26 first events, median lag 5, cum4 0.268 / cum12 0.6341, Greenwood bands h4 (0.133, 0.268, 0.404) and h12 (0.487, 0.634, 0.782), climatology cum4 0.168 / cum12 0.4409 with band (0.411, 0.441, 0.471), stress 33 entries / 258 person-weeks / 21 de-stresses / cum12 0.6364 / band (0.472, 0.636, 0.801), kill lines 3/8/19/31. Standing consistency check (sum of at-risk == person-weeks) asserted true everywhere. Determinism: a second run on a fresh fetch reproduced the full report byte-identically (all pre-2021 weeks long closed; the forming week cannot enter the design window).

## 2. Fetch and occupancy (pre-2021, unsealed)

Stage0c conventions throughout (sanity, repair, thin-volume policy identical to the NQ pipeline); fetched fresh 2026-11-14. GOLD GC=F: 6,001+ daily rows, 0 repairs; NIKKEI ^N225: 6,701 raw, 122 nulls dropped, 0 repairs; DAX ^GDAXI: 6,867 raw, 43 nulls dropped, 0 repairs. All three symbols usable; no substitution was needed. Rank-transfer cuts per market (each market's OWN pre-2021 sig_w distribution, frozen convention): GOLD LOW 0.016023 / HIGH 0.022098; NIKKEI 0.019622 / 0.027155; DAX 0.019349 / 0.026782. Occupancy tilings under the rank cuts are near-identical to NQ's by construction (27.5 / 30.6 / 41.9 percent within rounding). The NQ absolute cuts are reported as context only in the bookkeeping and were never used.

## 3. Compression arm: entry-anchored 12-week first-significant-week KM vs matched climatology

| Market | Entries | First events | Median lag | cum4 | cum12 [Greenwood 95%] | Climatology cum12 [95%] | GAP12 | Sign |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NQ (frozen, parity anchor) | 41 | 26 | 5 | 0.268 | 0.634 [0.487, 0.782] | 0.441 [0.411, 0.471] | +0.193 | positive |
| GOLD | 47 | 29 | 5 | 0.298 | 0.617 [0.478, 0.756] | 0.481 [0.451, 0.512] | +0.136 | positive |
| NIKKEI | 48 | 27 | 6 | 0.208 | 0.562 [0.422, 0.703] | 0.374 [0.345, 0.403] | +0.188 | positive |
| DAX | 50 | 34 | 6 | 0.240 | 0.680 [0.551, 0.809] | 0.465 [0.435, 0.495] | +0.215 | positive |

READINGS, recorded as design bookkeeping:

1. THE GAP SIGN IS POSITIVE IN ALL THREE MARKETS. The salience gap (entry cum12 minus own-climatology cum12) is +0.136 (GOLD), +0.188 (NIKKEI), +0.215 (DAX), against the frozen NQ +0.193. The one quantity the NQ definition audits established as robust (the gap, not the level) carries the same sign in every market tested so far, at magnitudes within the NQ grid's own cell range (0.106-0.222).
2. THE OCCUPANCY GATE PASSES EVERYWHERE: 47/48/50 compression entries, all above the pre-declared 10-entry minimum; no arm closed untested. Sample sizes are the same order as NQ's 41.
3. THE TIMING SHAPE TRANSFERS: median lags 5/6/6 against NQ's 5, with hazards front-loaded in all three (person-week tables in research/stage0x_stageA_report.json).
4. NAIVE DISJOINT-BAND SEPARATION IS DEFINITION-LOCAL HERE TOO, exactly as on NQ: under raw Greenwood bands, NIKKEI (0.422 > 0.403) and DAX (0.551 > 0.495) separate from their climatologies while GOLD (0.478 < 0.512) does not. On NQ this sentence already carries TWO attachments (it held in 4 of 11 definition cells and in 0 of 3 dependence-honest rival constructions); the same caveats transfer verbatim: the climatology bands treat ~1050 overlapping episodes as independent, and the entry bands treat ~50 episodes as independent while eras cluster. THE HONEST CROSS-MARKET OBJECT IS THE GAP AND THE POINTS, never the naive band-overlap sentence. No pooled claim is made: any pooled statement (e.g. "positive in all four") requires the family max-t across markets per amendment Section 5, and design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof, in any case.
5. PER-METAL/INDEX CAVEATS INHERITED: Gold is a continuous-futures series (roll splice); Nikkei and DAX are cash indices (basis vs the traded instrument, no volume for future flow layers); currency and holiday calendars differ; W-FRI aggregation is a convention. Episode dependence, small n (~50 entries, 26-34 events), no stationarity claim: inherited everywhere, same as NQ.

## 7. What this does and does not mean

Design bookkeeping on unsealed pre-2021 data cannot prove the hypothesis in any market (amendment Section 6). What it does establish: the compression-entry salience mechanism is NOT an artifact of the NQ instrument alone; its sign, timing shape and order of magnitude replicate in three markets with different vol regimes, currencies and calendars, under a definition transferred by rank with zero re-tuning. What decides the question in each market: that market's forward rows against its own frozen kill lines and climatology, booked at its own clock entries, beginning from signature. The forward-booking tooling for the three markets (mirroring smh_forecast/smh_score with per-market cuts and logs) is the next buildable step; the kill lines are already frozen so it can be scored from its first row.

# SMH Cycle v0: the post-print clock, the return lanes, and the actuarial table - proof given a measured timeline

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 42, PROGRESS block 50, on Andrea's "continue developing the hypothesis and proving it". Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; the forward protocol is unchanged. All constants below are recomputed and asserted by the smh_theorem.py selftest (verify_all's smh_theorem check; the bundle stays at 20 checks).

This layer answers the two questions the aftermath (docs/smh_aftermath_v0.md) left open, and then does the one thing that can honestly be done with the word "proof" on a design sample: it measures the TIMELINE of proof.

On proof, stated once and frozen: nothing computable on the 2000-2020 sample can prove the hypothesis - every statistic on that sample is bookkeeping by definition, and the holdout is spent. The proof object of this framework is the forward calibration record: rows logged at clock entries before outcomes, scored against outcomes, dying at the pre-declared kill lines. What design data CAN do is say how long proof takes. That is this layer's third table.

## 1. The post-print clock (KM to the NEXT significant week)

Kaplan-Meier from each significant week to the next significant week, censoring at the sample end. Two versions: from all 47 pre-2021 significant weeks, and from the 26 first events of compression entries specifically.

| clock | cum h4 | cum h12 (Greenwood 95%) | cum h26 | median |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| from any of the 47 prints | 0.149 | 0.299 [0.168, 0.430] | 0.650 [0.512, 0.788] | 19 weeks |
| from the 26 first events | 0.038 [0.000, 0.112] | 0.231 [0.069, 0.393] | 0.654 [0.471, 0.837] | 18 weeks |

Readings, frozen:

1. THE SUPPRESSION, SEPARATED. From the 26 first events, the probability of another print within 12 weeks is 0.231 [0.069, 0.393] - BELOW the climatology counterfactual 0.441 [0.411, 0.471] with disjoint bands. After an entry-conditioned print, the next print within the quarter is rarer than from a week picked at random. This is the lone-print finding (aftermath section 1) restated as a clock and carried against its counterfactual; the mirror image of the salience gradient (entry clocks above climatology at the cross, below it after the print). Caveats frozen: n = 26 starts, episode dependence everywhere, and the ENDOGENOUS BAR - the print raises sig_w (median 1.324x), so part of the suppression is mechanical; an absolute-vol yardstick is a different definition. Suggestive, mechanically assisted, stated with its bands.
2. THE QUIET AFTER THE DRAMA. Zero prints in the first 3 weeks after a first event (0 of 26; from any print the count is 6 of 47). The quiet-handoff pattern repeats at the event's exit.
3. PRINTS RECUR ONCE THE BAR SETTLES. By 26 weeks the cum reaches 0.650/0.654: the machine prints again within about half a year with two-thirds probability. The observer who just caught a print is not done observing; the observer waiting for the NEXT one waits a median 18-19 weeks.

## 2. The return lanes (how the machine comes home)

Of the 26 first events, the road to the next compression cross (1 censored at the sample end) splits:

| lane | count | median event-to-cross wait |
|---|---|---|
| DECAY-FIRST (cross before any fresh stress entry) | 14 | 11.5 weeks |
| STRESS-FIRST (a fresh stress entry intervenes) | 11 | 20.0 weeks |

The faster lane is the quiet one: when the machine slides home on vol decay alone it arrives in about 11 and a half weeks; when it goes through a fresh stress spell first, the trip takes nearly twice as long. The circle has two lanes and the boring lane is faster - the doctrine's erosion-not-catharsis finding in path form.

## 3. The actuarial table (the long-run lemma quantified)

In-sample empirical rates over the 1050 valid weeks (20.12 years). STATIONARITY IS NOT CLAIMED: the sample spans the dot-com bust and the GFC; the live rate belongs to the forward log, not to this table.

| quantity | design rate |
|---|---|
| compression entries (triggers) | 41 in 20.12 years = 2.04 per year, ~20.4 per decade |
| entry-conditioned first events | 26 in 20.12 years = 1.29 per year, ~12.9 per decade |
| significant weeks | 47 in 20.12 years = 2.34 per year, ~23.4 per decade |
| mean cycle between compression entries | 1050 / 41 = 25.6 weeks |
| region occupancy | compression 27.5% / mid 30.6% / stress 41.9% |

TILING CONSISTENCY ANCHOR (new standing check): the three frozen dwell tables tile the sample exactly - 41 spells x mean 7.05 + 66 x 4.86 + 33 x 13.33 = 1049.7 ~= 1050 = the raw occupancy count. The dwell tables, measured independently in session 35, sum to the whole calendar with no remainder. The selftest asserts this.

The actuarial chain: 2.04 triggers per year x 0.634 capture = 1.29 events per year, internally consistent with the direct count (26/20.12). The long-run lemma is now a QUANTITY: about one and a quarter entry-conditioned anticipated events per year across the cycle, about thirteen per decade, none of them dated.

## 4. The proof timeline (the honest answer to "proving it")

At the design trigger rate, the pre-declared kill-line gates arrive at:

| gate | scored rows needed | years at 2.04 triggers/year |
|---|---|---|
| first public gate | n = 10 | ~4.9 |
| | n = 20 | ~9.8 |
| | n = 40 | ~19.6 |
| | n = 60 | ~29.4 |

Readings, frozen:

1. PROOF IS FORWARD AND IT IS SLOW. The occurrence claim cannot die (or survive) in public for roughly five years; the decisive gates (20-40 rows) sit at 10-20 years. This is the measured cost of doing science on a market clock that triggers twice a year. Anyone demanding faster proof is demanding a different, dishonest machine.
2. THE RATE IS A DESIGN NUMBER, NOT A PROMISE. If the live trigger rate differs (regime drift, structural vol change), the timeline stretches or shrinks with it; the forward log measures the live rate as it accumulates. The kill lines themselves are row-counted, never clock-counted: the gates open when the rows exist, not when the calendar says so.
3. WHAT ACCELERATES NOTHING: more design statistics. Every additional table on 2000-2020 data moves nothing about proof; the design side is complete.

## 5. Caveats, frozen

1. n = 47 starts (all prints) and n = 26 (first events); every band above carries episode dependence; the KM treats overlapping episodes as independent and they are not.
2. The suppression is mechanically assisted by the endogenous 2.5 x sig_w bar (the print raises its own threshold); flagged beside the claim, and the disjoint-band statement is made only for the first-events clock, not the all-prints clock (whose band overlaps climatology's marginally: 0.430 vs 0.411).
3. The actuarial rates are in-sample empirical over a specific 20-year window; no stationarity, no projection, no forward content.
4. The kill-year column is arithmetic on the design rate (n / 2.04), not a forecast of when gates open.
5. No rule, probability, kill line, or protocol value changed in this layer; T, V, V4h untouched; nothing booked; the forward log stays empty by design.

## 6. Honest computation log

- The first scratch pass at the post-print Greenwood bands used (1 - S) where the SE of the cumulative needs S: the bands came out absurdly narrow ([0.131, 0.167] at h4 where the correct band is [0.047, 0.251]). Caught by sanity-checking the width against the at-risk counts before any constant was frozen; recomputed with the standard Greenwood form S * sqrt(sum d/(n(n-d))) used throughout the bundle; all constants above are from the corrected pass. The selftest now asserts the corrected values on every run.
- The quiet-3w constant (0 of 26) was cross-checked against the raw inter-arrival gaps (only 2 of 46 gaps are consecutive) before freezing.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_cycle_v0.md

# SMH DOSSIER v0 — the unifying clue dossier, criteria ordered by closeness to the anticipated event

Status: CONSOLIDATION DOCUMENT (18 Nov 2026, session 94, PROGRESS block 97), answering the owner's 18 Nov 2026 directive: "look historically all we saw and start building criteria list by closeness to actual event starting from price and then volume and then time to build a unifying clue dossier that gives enough to form a whole framework once."

Documentation only. Every number below is a frozen, already-registered design constant restated from the cited bundle documents; no new computation was performed, nothing was evaluated, nothing was booked, no sealed window was touched (NQ 2021+ holdout SPENT; cross-market 2021+ sealed; BTC 2021+ sealed). All design statistics are bookkeeping on the unsealed pre-2021 sample and never proof; the proof object is the forward calibration record alone, which is empty by design. Direction is refused everywhere in this dossier; nothing below predicts a sign.

## 0. The end goal, restated formally

The framework the owner wants is exactly four questions answered at a decision close:
1. WHEN is a big move likely? (the hazard)
2. HOW CLOSE in time can we get to it? (the timeliness margin)
3. WHICH WAY? (direction — refused until forward rows earn it)
4. WHAT DOES IT COST to be there? (exploitability — outside every registered claim)

The dossier assembles every measured clue the programme holds, ordered by closeness to the event within the owner's requested layering: price first, then volume, then time. Sections marked NULL are clues that were tested honestly and measured to carry nothing; they stay in the dossier because a unifying framework is defined as much by what it excludes as by what it includes.

## 1. PRICE LAYER (clues measured at the decision close — closeness: the trigger itself)

### 1.1 The compression cross — the programme's strongest clue
- Definition (frozen): sig_w, the weekly volatility measure, crosses down into the frozen compression region. One trigger, one event, one clock.
- The anticipated event E: a significant week, |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w, within 12 weeks.
- Measured occurrence: 0.634 (26/41 design windows), Greenwood band [0.487, 0.782], versus do-nothing climatology 0.441. Bands disjoint. GAP +0.193. (smh_hazard_v0, smh_proof_criteria_v0)
- Robustness: positive in every cell of the 11-cell definition-elasticity audit (gaps 0.106-0.222), and the frozen definition is deliberately NOT the grid optimum. (smh_robustness_v0, family_registry_v0)
- Cross-market, same rank-transferred rule, zero re-tuning (design windows): GOLD cum12 0.617 [0.478, 0.756] vs 0.481, gap +0.136; NIKKEI 0.562 [0.422, 0.703] vs 0.374, gap +0.188; DAX 0.680 [0.551, 0.809] vs 0.465, gap +0.215. The gap sign is positive in every market tested, within the NQ grid's own range. (smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0)
- Kill criteria: 3/8/19/31 events at 10/20/40/60 scored forward rows. (smh_proof_criteria_v0)

### 1.2 The stress trigger — the second, weaker clue
- Same construction on the other side: cross into the stress region. Design p0 0.636 (21/33), the SAME kill-line thresholds (round to the same 5% binomial quantile). Recorded honestly as the weaker clue: its edge over a coin is smaller than compression's edge over climatology. (smh_proof_criteria_v0)

### 1.3 Event anatomy — what the anticipated move actually is
- Median lag from the entry to the event: 5 weeks.
- Median overshoot of the event bar: 1.41x its own 2.5-sigma threshold; maximum 2.63x. The median significant week is a ~3.5-sigma week. "Anticipated big move" concretely means this: a threshold-crossing event of measured central size, not a point target.
- Median dwell of the compression spell: 4 weeks. The entry-anchored clock and the spell-anchored abort condition were reconciled by the competing-risks decomposition. (smh_event_anatomy_v0)

### 1.4 Timeliness — the ranked closeness ladder (weeks of forfeited capture)
- Enter at the cross: 0.634.
- Wait 4 quiet weeks, then enter: 0.500.
- Wait 6 quiet weeks: 0.348 (= (0.634 - 0.439)/(1 - 0.439)).
- Waiting gains nothing the clock does not already condition on, forfeits the front-loaded mass, and races the spell's own mortality (median dwell 4). THE COMPRESSION CROSS IS THE MEASURED CLOSEST APPROACH TO THE EVENT. Finer timing needs intraday data the programme does not hold. (smh_entry_theorem_v0, smh_methodology_review_v0)

### 1.5 Priced-ness — the clue's cheapness, measured and frozen
- Implied vol (VXN) at compression entries: median 16.47 versus 20.14 unconditional; the 25.8th percentile of its own distribution (mid entries the 46.3rd, stress entries the 67.4th; region occupancy 22.5 / 42.4 / 77.7). Level-cheap.
- But MEAN-priced: the variance risk premium at entries is +3.71 vol points (positive at 73.2% of entries) versus +4.55 (76.7%) unconditional. The strong unseen-ness claim — the market fails to price the coming expansion — is REFUTED at the mean in the design sample. The crowd's calm is still overpriced calm. (smh_unseen_v0; smh_priced_calm_v0 honesty retained: era confound severe, n = 21/20 across two decades)

## 2. VOLUME LAYER (the honest null layer — what volume did and did not deliver)

### 2.1 NULLS — carried in the dossier as exclusions
- H8 flow-fade (divTop), Amendment 3 signed: n = 32, wrong-signed, p 0.79. Volume divergence does not call tops in the future tense. (amendment3_h8_h9_topbottom)
- H9 climax: n = 3 events in 26 years. Too rare to be a clue. divBot: 0 events. (same)
- sp_z at-entry covariate: null, as pre-declared. The one prior sp_z entry-timing filter produced zero trades ever. (smh_spz_covariate_v0)
- AVFI in-sample direction path: 0.42 versus random-entry 0.465. The OHLCV-derived direction guess is not directional evidence. (smh_strategy_v0)
- Second-order VPVI flow (geometry proxy, session 92): not flow, close-free, reversal bars misread by construction. Display machinery only.
- Holdout battery: rule set T failed at the margin (0.575, p 0.069 vs 0.05); U3 failed too (0.418, p 0.116). Twenty-seven indicator variants dead in the H-number era.

### 2.2 What volume DOES carry, registered
- AVFI as a slow regime descriptor and sizing throttle — the armed-sizing verdict. Volume's measured job is exposure control, not entry timing and not direction.
- UNTESTED AND DATA-BLOCKED: the volume/Hawkes layer needs the broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export (Amendment 10, decision + export together); the options term-structure unseen-ness layer needs his data-source choice (Amendment 14 candidate, unsigned). These are the volume layer's two live levers, both waiting on the owner.

- T: weekly paper, Entry 1 booked, FLAT since 2026-10-30; next decision close Friday 2026-11-20.
- V4h: 11 booked rows, equity 99,092.88, audit CLEAN; no booking since Friday 2026-11-13 (the standing decay item, his task-system check).
- Amendment 9 macro booking: rehearsed green, never fired; first fire window Sunday 2026-11-22 23:45 Malta.

## 3. TIME LAYER (the calendar layer — actuarial, not dated prophecy)

### 3.1 Trigger actuarial
- ~2.04 triggers per year, ~1.29 anticipated events/year. ROWS ARE STABLE, YEARS ARE NOT: capture fraction 0.647 vs 0.625 across decade halves, while the trigger rate moves by a factor of 4.5. Regime range 0.7-3.2 triggers/year. Hot regime: first gate in ~3 years; cold: nearer 15. The kill lines are row-counted, never clock-counted. (smh_priced_calm_v0, smh_cycle_v0)

### 3.2 Proof schedule (the falsification spine of the whole framework)
- Compression kill lines: 3/8/19/31 events at 10/20/40/60 scored rows; false-kill (true 0.634 machine) 0.0335/0.0282/0.0290/0.0415; power against the measured climatology 0.2855/0.4465/0.7243/0.9046 (the falsificationist's fee is ~3-4% per gate; the cumulative one-run-in-eight true-machine death ~0.126 is the stated price of public falsifiability).
- Occurrence gates arrive roughly 5/10/20/29 years at the design rate; the readout schedule ~13/25/51 years.
- THE POSITIVE PROOF is exactly one statement: at least 57 of 104 resolved compression 12-week windows resolve with the event. Power 0.971, false-pass 0.018.
- Direction peeks (pre-declared, refused until then): n = 40 and n = 60 scored rows, roughly ~20 and ~29 years. (smh_proof_criteria_v0, smh_methodology_review_v0)

### 3.3 Cycle vocabulary — descriptive only
- The Copsey cycle ladders (82/164-week, 24/48-week, 196-month, 33-year): hypothesis generator for the parked H3; zero statistical evidence in that source, no nulls, no counts. In the dossier as vocabulary, never as a clue.

## 4. THE GAPS, ranked by what stands between the dossier and his end goal
1. DIRECTION. The largest gap, refused as a theorem. The design sample is spent; any direction rule built on it now is curve-fit wearing a costume. The two peeks are the only lawful path (~20/~29 years), unless a signed amendment brings NEW data types whose directional content can be tested on sealed windows.
2. FORWARD PROOF. Zero scored rows. The first gate is the binding next milestone on the road; the macro cross-market stream is the only instrument that can open gates before NQ's own clock.
3. PRICED-NESS. Level-cheap but mean-priced; whether the options layer refines this is exactly Amendment 14's question (his a/b/c data-source choice).
4. COST. Post-cost exploitability is outside every claim. No position-sizing claim in this dossier is tradeable advice; paper only.
5. INTRADAY TIMING. Closer-than-weekly entry needs L2/intraday data; the runbook exists, the download is his hands.

## 5. What can compress this calendar (all owner-side)
The decision queue (docs/decision_queue_v0.md) in suggested order: the C-W3 barrier-repair ratification; Amendment 13 (C-W1 BTC weekly); Amendment 14 (D-3 options unseen-ness) with the data-source choice; D-1; D-6; Amendment 10 with the broker/CME export; shelfed 6/7/8. Hands-only: the L2 download, the V4h task check, the TradingView paste cross-check, the export checklist 093.

## 6. What this dossier is
A unifying framework exists in this dossier as a falsification architecture: one price trigger per side with measured hazards and kill lines, a measured timeliness ladder, an honest volume-null layer, an actuarial time layer, and a public proof statement. It is not a direction system, and by the central theorem it cannot become one by looking harder at the same spent history. What it can do, and only forward time or the owner's new data can grant, is earn the missing layers one scored row at a time — starting with the first gate, and for direction, the first peek.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_edge_route_v0.md (10600 bytes)
# SMH Edge Route v0: how edge will be found, and the cross-market mould

Status: DEVELOPMENT DOCUMENT. This is the written answer to the standing question "how will edge actually be found; how does SMH mould into prediction by inevitabilities criteria it uniquely achieves, applicable across all markets." It is a design statement grounded in the frozen protocol and the now-measured Stage A cross-market bookkeeping (docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0.md, GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX gaps +0.136/+0.188/+0.215 against frozen NQ +0.193). It creates no permission, no claim, and no evaluation; every number it cites is design bookkeeping or a frozen constant.

## 1. The core commitment: edge is actuarial, not oracular

SMH does not predict prices. It predicts a recurrence frequency: given a compression entry (or stress entry) on a market's own clock, the probability that a first significant week lands within 12 weeks is above that market's matched climatology by a frozen margin. The prediction object is a HAZARD, not a level and not a direction. Edge is found if and only if forward booked rows, scored against each market's own pre-frozen kill lines and climatology, survive. That is an actuarial edge: it monetizes like an insurance book (many small calibrated bets on event timing), not like a forecast of where the market goes.

The "inevitability" is not a claim that the event must happen. It is a claim about the RELIABILITY OF RECURRENCE STATISTICS under a definition that does not need retuning per market: compression episodes are followed by significant weeks at rates materially above unconditional climatology, in every market tested at design stage so far, under cuts transferred by rank with zero re-tuning. What is "inevitable" is the shape of the conditional clock, not any single event.

## 2. The inevitability criteria SMH uniquely achieves, and why they transfer across markets

The hypothesis was built to satisfy five criteria simultaneously; each one is what makes the cross-market mould honest rather than curve-fit:

1. ACTUARIAL RECURRENCE. The claim is a frequency ratio (entry cum12 vs matched climatology cum12) with pre-frozen kill lines, evaluable at small n, one market at a time. It does not need a long profitable track record to be falsified; ten forward rows can already breach a kill line (GOLD n=10 line is 3; NIKKEI is 2). Most market hypotheses cannot state a death schedule in advance; SMH's is public before the first row.
2. EDGE-LOCAL SALIENCE GAP. The audited quantity is the GAP, not the level: entry-anchored event frequency minus climatology. The gap is the one quantity the NQ definition-grid audits showed robust (it survived the definition grid; naive band-overlap did not survive rival constructions). Stage A shows the gap sign is positive in GOLD, NIKKEI and DAX at magnitudes inside the NQ grid's own cell range. A level would be market-specific; a gap is portable because it is measured against each market's own unconditional baseline, whatever that baseline is.
3. HAZARD TIMING, NOT POINT FORECASTS. The prediction is a survival curve (front-loaded hazards, median lag 5-6 weeks, cum4/cum12 split), which is tradeable information for option buyers, calendar spreaders, and risk managers even with direction refused. Timing claims are also cheap to falsify: the 12-week window closes mechanically, censored rows included, no discretion.
4. DIRECTION REFUSAL. SMH deliberately refuses to predict direction on design data (direction is forward-only, two peeks per market, registered in advance). This is what makes the design stage honest: no cell of the cross-market design was selected for direction performance, so nothing is curve-fit to up/down asymmetry. It also forces the edge to live in timing and calibration, where the actuarial structure is strongest.
5. PUBLIC FALSIFIABILITY WITH PRE-FROZEN CONSTANTS. Cuts by rank, kill lines, climatology bands, p0 values, holdout boundaries: all frozen in dated signed documents before the first forward row, per market. Cross-market, the family max-t rule is declared in advance. No other market hypothesis Andrea works with has this property; it is the anti-overfitting criterion, and Stage A honored it (lines frozen in the addendum the same day the results were computed, before any forward row exists anywhere).

The phrase "criteria it uniquely achieves" is thus operationally precise: the five criteria are simultaneously satisfiable by an actuarial recurrence claim and are NOT satisfiable by a direction-forecast or pattern-recognition claim (which cannot pre-freeze a death schedule, cannot refuse direction, and cannot transfer across markets without re-tuning). That is the mould.

## 3. How the definition transfers: the cross-market mould

1. RANK TRANSFER, NOT VALUE TRANSFER. A new market gets cuts at the same PERCENTILES of its own pre-2021 sig_w distribution (27.52nd / 58.10th), not the NQ values. This is why "same recipe" is not "same parameters": GOLD's LOW cut is 0.0160, NIKKEI's 0.0196, DAX's 0.0193; each is that market's own rank location.
2. PER-MARKET EVERYTHING. Each market gets its own Stage A (design bookkeeping on its own unsealed pre-2021 window), its own sealed 2021+ holdout, its own climatology, its own kill lines (GOLD 3/8/19/30, NIKKEI 2/7/16/26, DAX 3/9/21/34 at n=10/20/40/60), its own two direction peeks, its own forward log. Nothing pools.
3. FAMILY MULTIPLICITY CONTROL. Any claim stated across markets ("positive gap in all four") is evaluated under the family max-t across all four; design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof, everywhere.
4. THE FORWARD LOG IS THE PROOF SURFACE. Design windows can never prove anything (amendment Section 6); they exist to freeze constants and to make sure the mechanism is not instrument-specific BEFORE asking for forward rows. The mould is: Stage 0x occupancy, Stage A bookkeeping + kill-line freeze, then forward rows at clock entries, scored against the frozen schedule.

## 4. How edge will actually be found: the route, step by step

STEP 1 (DONE THIS SESSION): machinery validated (NQ parity gate passed first run, determinism verified), Stage A bookkeeping on GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX, kill lines frozen per market in the addendum. The mechanism's sign, timing shape and gap magnitude replicate at design stage; the question is now purely forward.
STEP 2 (NEXT BUILD): cross-market forward-booking tooling, mirroring the NQ forecast/score scripts with per-market cuts, logs and kill lines. Each Sunday, each market's clock either fires an entry (a row is booked with direction refused) or does not. Logs start empty BY DESIGN.
STEP 3 (ACCUMULATION): rows accumulate only at the market's own clock speed. With ~41-50 entries per decade per market at design density, four markets book roughly 4 rows per year in total (this is a slow actuarial book; the 10-20 h/week cap is not the constraint, the clock is).
STEP 4 (SCORING): at each n anchor (10/20/40/60), each market's arm either survives or breaches its kill line and closes UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN (the design cum12 was wrong about that market). Censored rows count as non-events, no exceptions. Two direction peeks per market, registered in advance, tell whether the timing edge carries an up/down asymmetry worth a second, separately-amended claim; the peeks are diagnostics, never entry rules.
STEP 5 (THE EDGE ITSELF): if arms survive, the durable private asset is the CALIBRATION RECORD: a multi-market book of survival curves with pre-frozen constants and a public falsification history. That record is what a systematic options program (buying 4-to-12-week premium at compression entries), a risk desk (pre-positioning for regime transitions), or a prop calendar-spreading book consumes. The tradable expression of an actuarial timing edge is optionality, not futures direction.
STEP 6 (ACCELERATION IS REPLICATION, NOT TUNING): the only honest way to get more evidence faster is more markets under the same mould (Amendment 12 draft: ES, ZN, DX, BTC with the claims-ledger C-W1 resolution and the 24/7 calendar caveat), each paying its own Stage A and its own kill lines. Re-tuning a failing market's constants is forbidden by the protocol and would destroy the falsifiability criterion that makes the edge credible to any counterparty.

## 5. What edge will NOT be (negative commitments, standing)

1. No new entry rules mined from design data (design statistics are bookkeeping; a seductive cell in any market's design window is a trap, not a strategy).
2. No direction strategy until the registered peeks say so under their own future amendment; direction refusal is load-bearing for honesty, not a limitation to optimize away.
3. No pooling that survives without family max-t; no cross-market average claim of any kind.
4. No reopening of closed arms, no editing of frozen constants, no exceptions for interesting markets. The kill lines are the product; bending them destroys the only thing SMH uniquely owns, which is the public pre-frozen falsification record.
5. No claim that design-stage replication (GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX gaps positive) proves anything: three positive design gaps raise the prior for the forward test; they cannot substitute for it.

## 6. The honest reading of where the programme stands

NQ's own positive proof (57-of-104) is spent and untouched; its two direction peeks are pending per their register. Cross-market, the mechanism now has four design windows with positive gaps and zero re-tuning, frozen per-market death schedules, and sealed holdouts. If the forward rows confirm at even one new market, the claim "recurrence above climatology under rank-transferred cuts" stops being an NQ fact and becomes a market fact. That is the mould, and the edge route is nothing more than running it forward without bending it.

Literature anchors (for the concepts, not for any claim here): the actuarial object is a survival curve in the Kaplan-Meier (1958) and Cox (1972) tradition; the climatology-matching discipline parallels the conditional-volatility literature (Cont 2001 stylized facts; Clark 1973 subordination; the realized-volatility line through Meddahi-Paranquet-Renault 2012 and the Hawkes/branching line through Filimonov-Sornette 2012 and Ait-Sahalia et al. 2010-2013); the multiplicity discipline is White (2000) reality checks and data-snooping, with Lo (2002) on Sharpe-ratio inference pitfalls; the "design statistics are not proof" discipline is McLean-Pontiff (2016) on post-publication decay; regime-clock economics is Hamilton (1989); the skepticism about long-memory spuriousness is Diebold-Inoue (2001).
